** PRIMITIVE-TYPE :compiled:function:class:

#+begin_example

PRIMITIVE-TYPE names a compiled function:

  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T) *)
  Source file: SYS:SRC;COMPILER;GENERIC;PRIMTYPE.LISP

PRIMITIVE-TYPE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-C:PRIMITIVE-TYPE>:
  Class precedence-list: PRIMITIVE-TYPE, STRUCTURE-OBJECT, SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT,
                         T
  Direct superclasses: STRUCTURE-OBJECT
  No subclasses.
  Slots:
    SB-C::NAME
      Type: SYMBOL
      Initform: NIL
    SB-C::SCS
      Type: LIST
      Initform: NIL
    SB-C::SPECIFIER
      Type: (OR SYMBOL LIST)
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;COMPILER;GENERIC;PRIMTYPE.LISP
- SYS:SRC;COMPILER;VOP.LISP
:end:

  Slots:

      Type: SYMBOL
      Initform: NIL

      Initform: :NON-PACKED

      Type: SB-INT:INDEX
      Initform: 0

#+end_example

** NOTE-THIS-LOCATION :compiled:function:
:PROPERTIES:
:ID: SB-C:NOTE-THIS-LOCATION
:ALLOC: HEAP IMMOBILE
:END:
 
#+begin_example

NOTE-THIS-LOCATION names a compiled function:
  Lambda-list: (VOP KIND)
  Derived type: (FUNCTION (T T) (VALUES SB-C::LOCATION-INFO &OPTIONAL))
  Documentation:
    NOTE-THIS-LOCATION VOP Kind
    Note that the current code location is an interesting (to the debugger)
    location of the specified Kind. VOP is the VOP responsible for this code.
    This VOP must specify some non-null :SAVE-P value (perhaps :COMPUTE-ONLY)so
    that the live set is computed.
  Source file: SYS:SRC;COMPILER;DEBUG-DUMP.LISP

#+end_example

:definitions:
- SYS:SRC;COMPILER;DEBUG-DUMP.LISP
:end:

                         T
  Direct superclasses: STRUCTURE-OBJECT
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    SB-C::%MEM-SPACE
      Type: (MEMBER NIL :DYNAMIC :IMMOBILE :AUTO)
      Initform: NIL

      Type: (MEMBER NIL :TOPLEVEL :COMPLEX-TOPLEVEL :INITIAL :DELETED)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-C::HEAD
      Type: SB-C::CBLOCK
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)
    SB-C::TAIL
      Type: SB-C::CBLOCK
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)

#+begin_example

JUMP-TABLE names the structure-class #<STRUCTURE-CLASS SB-C:JUMP-TABLE>:
  Class precedence-list: SB-C:JUMP-TABLE, SB-C::MULTIPLE-SUCCESSORS-NODE,
                         SB-C::NODE, SB-INT:SSET-ELEMENT, STRUCTURE-OBJECT,
                         SB-PCL::SLOT-OBJECT, T
  Direct superclasses: SB-C::MULTIPLE-SUCCESSORS-NODE
  No subclasses.
  Sealed.
  Slots:
    NUMBER
      Type: (OR SB-INT:INDEX NULL)
      Initform: (WHEN (BOUNDP 'SB-C:*COMPILATION*)
                  (INCF (SB-C::SSET-COUNTER SB-C:*COMPILATION*)))
    SB-C::REOPTIMIZE
      Type: BOOLEAN
      Initform: T
    SB-C::NEXT
      Type: (OR SB-C::CTRAN NULL)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-C::PREV
      Type: (OR SB-C::CTRAN NULL)
      Initform: NIL
    SB-KERNEL:LEXENV
      Type: SB-KERNEL:LEXENV
      Initform: SB-C:*LEXENV*
    SB-C::SOURCE-PATH
      Type: LIST
      Initform: SB-C::*CURRENT-PATH*
    SB-C::TAIL-P
      Type: BOOLEAN
      Initform: NIL
    SB-INT:INDEX
      Type: SB-C::LVAR
      Initform: (SB-INT:MISSING-ARG)
    SB-C::TARGETS
      Type: LIST
      Initform: NIL

JUMP-TABLE names a dependent optimization policy quality:
  (undocumented)

#+end_example

- SYS:SRC;COMPILER;IR1-TRANSLATORS.LISP
